| ai_credits_exhausted_body | AI-powered features are now disabled for this organization. To re-enable them, please contact {0} to add more credits. | この組織のAI機能は現在無効になっています。再度有効にするには、{0}にご連絡いただき、クレジットの追加をお申し込みください。 | Body paragraph explaining that AI features are disabled and how to re-enable them. {0} is the sales contact email address. |

| ai_credits_exhausted_intro | Organization {0} has exhausted its AI credits. | 組織{0}のAIクレジットが不足しました。 | Intro paragraph for AI credits exhaustion notification. {0} is the organization name. |
| ai_credits_exhausted_subject | [{0}] AI credits exhausted for "{1}" | [{0}] 「{1}」のAIクレジットが不足しています | Subject for email sent to organization admins when AI credits are exhausted. {0} is the product name and {1} is the organization name. |
| ai_credits_expired_body | These credits were unused and have been removed from the organization's balance. To purchase more credits, go to Billing. | これらのクレジットは未使用のまま組織の残高から差し引かれました。クレジットを追加購入するには、請求ページに移動してください。 | Body paragraph explaining the expired credits were removed and how to buy more, via the organization's Billing page. |
| ai_credits_expired_cta | Go to Billing | 請求ページへ | Call-to-action button text for the AI credits expiration email, linking to the organization's Billing page. |
| ai_credits_expired_intro | {0} AI credits of organization {1} have reached their expiration date. | 組織{1}のAIクレジット{0}が有効期限に達しました。 | Intro paragraph for AI credits expiration notification. {0} is the number of expired credits and {1} is the organization name. |
| ai_credits_expired_subject | [{0}] AI credits expired for "{1}" | [{0}] 「{1}」のAIクレジットの有効期限が切れました | Subject for email sent to organization admins when AI credits expire. {0} is the product name and {1} is the organization name. |
| ai_credits_order_status_canceled | canceled | キャンセル済み | Status of an AI credits order that was canceled, inserted as {1} in "Order {0} was marked as {1}". |
| ai_credits_order_status_chargeback | charged back | チャージバック済み | Status of an AI credits order whose card payment was reclaimed by the cardholder's bank after a lost chargeback dispute, inserted as {1} in "Order {0} was marked as {1}". |
| ai_credits_order_status_refund | refunded | 返金済み | Status of an AI credits order that was refunded, inserted as {1} in "Order {0} was marked as {1}". |
| ai_credits_order_status_reversed | reversed | 取り消し済み | Status of an AI credits order whose authorized payment was reversed, inserted as {1} in "Order {0} was marked as {1}". |
| ai_credits_refund_deducted_detail | Order {0} was marked as {1}, so {2} credits were deducted. Current balance: {3} credits. | 注文{0}は{1}としてマークされたため、{2}クレジットが差し引かれました。現在の残高：{3}クレジット。 | Detail paragraph explaining the deduction. {0} is the order reference, {1} is the localized refund/cancel status the order transitioned to (see ai_credits_order_status_*), {2} is the locale-formatted credits deducted, {3} is the locale-formatted current wallet balance. |
| ai_credits_refund_deducted_intro | AI credits previously granted to organization {0} have been reversed. | 組織{0}に以前付与されたAIクレジットが取り消されました。 | Intro paragraph for the AI credits deduction notification. {0} is the organization name. |
| ai_credits_refund_deducted_subject | [{0}] AI credits adjusted for "{1}" | [{0}]「{1}」のAIクレジットが調整されました | Subject for email sent to organization admins when previously granted AI credits are deducted because the order was refunded/canceled after being processed as a purchase. {0} is the product name and {1} is the organization name. |
| ai_credits_topup_skipped_detail | Order {0} had already been marked as {1} before the purchase confirmation arrived, so no credits were added. Current balance: {2} credits. | 注文{0}は購入確認が届く前に既に{1}としてマークされていたため、クレジットは追加されませんでした。現在の残高：{2}クレジット。 | Detail paragraph explaining why the top-up was skipped. {0} is the order reference, {1} is the localized refund/cancel status the order already had (see ai_credits_order_status_*), {2} is the locale-formatted current wallet balance. |
| ai_credits_topup_skipped_intro | An AI credits purchase for organization {0} was not applied. | 組織{0}のAIクレジット購入は適用されませんでした。 | Intro paragraph for the skipped AI credits top-up notification. {0} is the organization name. |
| ai_credits_topup_skipped_subject | [{0}] AI credits top-up skipped for "{1}" | [{0}]「{1}」のAIクレジットのチャージがスキップされました | Subject for email sent to organization admins when an AI credits purchase's top-up is skipped because the order was already refunded/canceled. {0} is the product name and {1} is the organization name. |

| cannot_lock_auxiliary_file | Cannot edit an auxiliary task file. To make it editable, open it in a web browser and then click the "Start Editing" button. | 補助タスクファイルを編集できません。編集可能にするには、Web ブラウザーで開き、[編集開始] ボタンをクリックします。 | Message to be presented when cannot lock an auxiliary task file. |

| organization.ai.credits.batches.column.credits | Credits | クレジット | Column header: a credit batch's remaining balance, or for an expired batch the amount that expired unused |

| organization.ai.credits.batches.column.purchased | Purchase date | 購入日 | Column header: date a credit batch was granted |

| organization.ai.credits.batches.expires.soon | Expires soon | まもなく期限切れ | Label next to a credit batch's expiration date when it expires within 30 days |
| organization.ai.credits.batches.footnote | Note: only credits that expired within the last 6 months are shown here. | 注: ここには過去6か月以内に期限切れになったクレジットのみが表示されます。 | Footnote below the credit batches table clarifying that only recently expired batches are listed |
| organization.ai.credits.batches.load.failed | Failed to load credit batches. | クレジット バッチの読み込みに失敗しました。 | Error label when the credit batches table failed to load |
| organization.ai.credits.batches.loading | Loading credit batches... | クレジット バッチを読み込み中... | Loading label for the credit batches table |
| organization.ai.credits.batches.status.active | Active | アクティブ | Status chip label for a credit batch that is neither expiring soon nor expired; Simplified user-facing status for active subscriptions |
| organization.ai.credits.batches.subtitle | See balances and expiration dates | 残高と有効期限を表示 | Subtitle next to the credit batches section's expand/collapse control |
| organization.ai.credits.batches.title | AI credit batches | AI クレジット バッチ | Title of the collapsible credit batches section on the Billing page |
| organization.ai.credits.billing.note | To buy more AI credits, go to | AI クレジットを追加購入するには、次へ移動: | Note in organization settings linking to the billing page for purchasing credits |
| organization.ai.credits.buy.button | Buy AI Credits | AI クレジットを購入 | Button label to purchase an AI credits pack; Title of the dialog for purchasing AI credits |
| organization.ai.credits.buy.dialog.checkout | Proceed to Checkout | チェックアウトへ進む | Checkout button label in the buy AI credits dialog |
| organization.ai.credits.buy.dialog.credits.label | AI credits × 1M | AI クレジット × 100万 | Label for the units input in the buy dialog (1 unit = 1M credits) |
| organization.ai.credits.buy.dialog.description | Choose how many units to purchase. Each unit equals 1,000,000 AI credits. Credits will be added to your organization balance after payment. | 購入するユニット数を選択してください。1 ユニット = 1,000,000 AI クレジットです。支払い後、クレジットが組織の残高に追加されます。 | Description text in the buy AI credits dialog. 1 unit = 1,000,000 credits. |
| organization.ai.credits.buy.dialog.total | You will receive {total} (million) credits. | {total} 百万クレジットが付与されます。 | Shows total credits that will be added to the balance based on the selected unit count |
| organization.ai.credits.buy.dialog.validity.note | Purchased credits are valid for {days} days from the purchase date. | 購入したクレジットは購入日から{days}日間有効です。 | Note about how long purchased credits stay usable before they expire. {days} is the configured validity period in days. |
| organization.ai.credits.context | Credits are shared across all AI-powered features in your organization. | クレジットは組織内のすべての AI 機能で共有されます。 | Short explanation of how organization AI credits work |
| organization.ai.credits.disabled.exhausted | AI features are disabled. Re-enabling AI will not restore access until more credits are added. | AI 機能は無効です。クレジットが追加されるまで、AI を再有効化してもアクセスは復元されません。 | Info shown when AI features are disabled and the wallet has no credits left |
| organization.ai.credits.disabled.info | AI features are disabled. Your remaining credits are preserved and will not be consumed until you turn AI features back on. | AI 機能は無効です。残りのクレジットは保持され、AI 機能を再度有効にするまで消費されません。 | Info shown when AI features are disabled but credits remain in the wallet |
| organization.ai.credits.documentation.link | Learn how AI credits work | AI クレジットの仕組みを確認 | Link to Content Fusion documentation explaining how AI credits work |
| organization.ai.credits.exhausted.warning | Your organization has no AI credits left. AI-powered features are unavailable until more credits are added. | 組織の AI クレジットがなくなりました。クレジットが追加されるまで AI 機能は利用できません。 | Warning when organization AI credits are exhausted |
| organization.ai.credits.load.failed | Failed to load AI credits. | AI クレジットの読み込みに失敗しました。 | Error label when AI credits failed to load |
| organization.ai.credits.loading | Loading AI credits... | AI クレジットを読み込み中... | Loading label for AI credits section |
| organization.ai.credits.section.title | AI credits | AI クレジット | Subheading for the AI credits block under AI features settings |
| organization.ai.credits.trial.purchase.disabled | AI credits can be purchased once your organization is on a paid subscription plan. | AI クレジットは、組織が有料サブスクリプションプランに移行した後に購入できます。 | Message shown instead of buy buttons when the organization is on a trial subscription |
| organization.ai.usage | AI Usage | AI 使用状況 | Title of the organization admin page for AI credit limits and usage; also its navigation rail entry |
| organization.ai.usage.at.limit | {count} at limit | 上限到達: {count} | Toggle above the AI usage table that lists only the members who used up their spending limit; {count} is how many did |
| organization.ai.usage.at.limit.show.only | Show only users at their spending limit | 使用上限に達したユーザーのみ表示 | Tooltip of the at-limit filter chip above the AI usage table while it is off |
| organization.ai.usage.clear.filters | Clear filters | フィルターをクリア | Link that turns off every filter of the AI usage table |
| organization.ai.usage.credits.balance | Credits balance | クレジット残高 | Label of the prepaid AI credits balance shown below the organization monthly limit |
| organization.ai.usage.credits.balance.below.limit | Less than the remaining monthly spending limit | 残りの月間使用上限を下回っています | Warning beside the prepaid credits balance when it is smaller than what the organization monthly limit still allows this month |
| organization.ai.usage.credits.balance.runs.out | Credits balance runs out here | ここでクレジット残高がなくなります | Tooltip of the mark on the organization usage bar at the point where the prepaid credits balance is used up |
| organization.ai.usage.credits.per.month | credits / month | クレジット / 月 | Unit shown next to a monthly AI credit limit |
| organization.ai.usage.credits.used | {used} credits used | {used} クレジット使用済み | Organization consumption this month when it has no limit to show it against; {used} is a credit amount |
| organization.ai.usage.custom.limit.note | This custom spending limit overrides the {role} default until you choose “Use role default”. | このカスタム使用上限は、「ロールの既定値を使用する」を選択するまで {role} の既定値よりも優先されます。 | Explains precedence of a member limit over the role default and how to go back to it; {role} is a platform role name, and the quoted text is the label of the organization.ai.usage.use.role.default option |
| organization.ai.usage.edit.author.role.limit | Edit Author monthly spending limit | 著者の月間使用上限を編集 | Title of the dialog that changes the Author role default monthly AI credit limit |
| organization.ai.usage.edit.author.role.limit.description | This is the default monthly spending limit applied to every user with the Author role, unless a custom spending limit is set for their account. | これは、アカウントに独自の使用上限が設定されていない限り、著者ロールを持つすべてのユーザーに適用される既定の月間使用上限です。 | Explains what the Author role default limit does |
| organization.ai.usage.edit.contributor.role.limit | Edit Contributor monthly spending limit | コントリビューターの月間使用上限を編集 | Title of the dialog that changes the Contributor role default monthly AI credit limit |
| organization.ai.usage.edit.contributor.role.limit.description | This is the default monthly spending limit applied to every user with the Contributor role, unless a custom spending limit is set for their account. | これは、アカウントに独自の使用上限が設定されていない限り、コントリビューターロールを持つすべてのユーザーに適用される既定の月間使用上限です。 | Explains what the Contributor role default limit does |
| organization.ai.usage.edit.limit.for | Edit spending limit for {name} | {name} の使用上限を編集 | Accessible name of the per-member edit button, read by screen readers; {name} is the member's name |
| organization.ai.usage.edit.organization.limit | Edit organization monthly spending limit | 組織の月間使用上限を編集 | Title of the dialog that changes the organization monthly AI credit limit |
| organization.ai.usage.edit.spending.limit | Edit spending limit | 使用上限を編集 | Tooltip of the per-member edit button in the AI usage table |
| organization.ai.usage.edit.user.limit | Edit user monthly spending limit | ユーザーの月間使用上限を編集 | Title of the dialog that changes one member's monthly AI credit limit |
| organization.ai.usage.limit.custom | Custom | カスタム | Badge marking a member limit set specifically for that member |
| organization.ai.usage.limit.invalid | Enter an amount greater than 0, with at most {decimals} decimals. | 0 より大きい金額を、小数点以下 {decimals} 桁以内で入力してください。 | Guidance under a rejected monthly limit field; {decimals} is the number of decimals stored |
| organization.ai.usage.limits | Spending limits | 使用上限 | Heading of the section where monthly AI credit limits are configured |
| organization.ai.usage.limits.description | Monthly AI credits spending limits for your organization and for each user role. | 組織および各ユーザーロールの AI クレジットの月間使用上限です。 | Tooltip explaining what the Spending limits section configures |
| organization.ai.usage.load.failed | Failed to load AI usage. | AI 使用状況の読み込みに失敗しました。 | Error label when the AI usage overview failed to load |
| organization.ai.usage.member.email.and.role | {email} · {role} | {email} · {role} | Identifies the member being edited; {email} is their address and {role} their platform role |
| organization.ai.usage.member.used.of.limit | {used} / {limit} | {used} / {limit} | A member's consumption against their cap in the usage table; both are credit amounts |
| organization.ai.usage.no.limit | No spending limit | 使用上限なし | Shown in place of an amount when no monthly AI credit limit is set |
| organization.ai.usage.no.matching.members | No users match these filters. | これらのフィルターに一致するユーザーはいません。 | Shown in place of the AI usage table rows when the active filters leave none |
| organization.ai.usage.no.members | No users with an Author or Contributor role, and nobody used AI credits this month. | 著者またはコントリビューターのロールを持つユーザーはおらず、今月は誰も AI クレジットを使用していません。 | Shown when the organization has no Author or Contributor and nobody used AI credits |
| organization.ai.usage.no.members.no.contributor | No users with an Author role, and nobody used AI credits this month. | 著者のロールを持つユーザーはおらず、今月は誰も AI クレジットを使用していません。 | Shown when the organization has no Author and nobody used AI credits, where the Contributor role is unavailable |
| organization.ai.usage.no.organization.limit.description | No monthly spending limit is enforced. Users consume credits directly from your organization's total available credits until that balance runs out. | 月間使用上限は適用されません。ユーザーは組織の利用可能なクレジット残高がなくなるまで、そこから直接クレジットを消費します。 | Explains what happens when the organization has no monthly AI credit cap |
| organization.ai.usage.no.role.limit.description | Users with the {role} role can spend credits until the organization's spending limit is reached or its available credits run out. | {role} ロールのユーザーは、組織の使用上限に達するか、組織の利用可能なクレジットがなくなるまでクレジットを使用できます。 | Explains what happens when a role has no default monthly AI credit cap; {role} is a platform role name |
| organization.ai.usage.resets.on | Resets {date} — in {days, plural, one {# day} other {# days}} | {date} にリセットされます — あと {days} 日 | When the monthly usage limits restart; {date} is the reset date and {days} the days left |
| organization.ai.usage.resets.tomorrow | Resets {date} — tomorrow | {date} にリセットされます — 明日 | When the monthly usage limits restart, on the eve of the reset; {date} is the reset date |
| organization.ai.usage.role.default.is | {limit} credits / month — the current default for the {role} role | {limit} クレジット / 月 — {role} ロールの現在の既定値 | The limit a member falls back to; {limit} is a credit amount and {role} a platform role name |
| organization.ai.usage.role.default.is.none | No spending limit — the {role} role has no monthly default | 使用上限なし — {role} ロールには月間の既定値がありません | Shown when the member's role has no default limit; {role} is a platform role name |
| organization.ai.usage.role.defaults | Default spending limit per role | ロールごとのデフォルト使用上限 | Heading of the monthly AI credits limits every Author and Contributor gets by default, one per role |
| organization.ai.usage.role.limit.note | Changing this value does not affect users who already have a custom spending limit. | この値を変更しても、すでに独自の使用上限が設定されているユーザーには影響しません。 | Warns that changing a role default leaves members with their own limit untouched |
| organization.ai.usage.set.custom.limit | Set a custom spending limit | カスタム使用上限を設定する | Dialog option that gives one member a limit of their own |
| organization.ai.usage.set.monthly.limit | Set a monthly spending limit | 月間使用上限を設定する | Dialog option that caps monthly AI credit spending at a typed amount |
| organization.ai.usage.set.monthly.limit.description | The organization can spend up to this many credits each month. Usage resets at the start of every calendar month. | 組織は毎月この数までのクレジットを使用できます。使用量は暦月の初めにリセットされます。 | Explains the organization monthly limit option |
| organization.ai.usage.show.all.users | Show all users | すべてのユーザーを表示 | Tooltip of the at-limit filter chip above the AI usage table while it is on |
| organization.ai.usage.showing | Showing {shown} of {total} | {total} 件中 {shown} 件を表示 | How many members of the AI usage table the active filters leave; {shown} and {total} are member counts |
| organization.ai.usage.table.role | Role | 役割 | Column header for the platform role of a member in the AI credits usage table; Column header for project member role |
| organization.ai.usage.table.usage | Usage this month | 今月の使用量 | Column header for the AI credits a member consumed in the current month |
| organization.ai.usage.table.user | User | ユーザー | Column header for the member identity in the AI credits usage table |
| organization.ai.usage.usage | Usage by user | ユーザー別の使用状況 | Heading of the section listing each member's AI credits usage |
| organization.ai.usage.usage.description | Every user with an Author or Contributor role, plus anyone else who used AI credits this month. | 著者またはコントリビューターのロールを持つすべてのユーザーと、今月 AI クレジットを使用したその他のユーザーです。 | Explains which users the AI Credits Usage table lists |
| organization.ai.usage.usage.description.no.contributor | Every user with an Author role, plus anyone else who used AI credits this month. | 著者のロールを持つすべてのユーザーと、今月 AI クレジットを使用したその他のユーザーです。 | Explains which users the AI Credits Usage table lists, where the Contributor role is unavailable |
| organization.ai.usage.use.role.default | Use role default | ロールの既定値を使用する | Dialog option that hands a member back to the limit of their role |
| organization.ai.usage.used.short | {used} used | {used} 使用済み | Consumption shown beside a monthly AI credit limit; {used} is a credit amount |
| organization.ai.usage.used.this.month | {used} credits used this month | 今月は {used} クレジットを使用しました | Credits consumed this month when there is no limit to measure them against; {used} is a credit amount |
| organization.cancel.deletion.error | Failed to cancel organization deletion | 組織の削除のキャンセルに失敗しました | Error message presented when canceling organization deletion fails. |
| organization.cannot.be.accessed | You cannot access this organization | この組織にアクセスできません | Generic anti-enumeration message when user cannot access an organization (to prevent organization enumeration). |
| organization.change.menu.find.by.email | Find my organizations by email | メールで自分の組織一覧を探す |  |

To request access, email {supportEmail} and include your organization name. | 組織の AI 機能は無効になっています。このページから再有効化することはできません。アクセスをリクエストするには、{supportEmail} にメールし、組織名を記載してください。 | Info shown when a cloud administrator has disabled AI for the organization |
| organization.compliance.ai.description | Content Fusion integrates with Oxygen AI Positron so authors can use AI to enhance documentation creation. | Content Fusion は Oxygen AI Positron と連携し、著者が AI でドキュメント作成を強化できます。 | Short presentation of AI capabilities: Content Fusion integration with Oxygen AI Positron |
| organization.compliance.ai.section.title | AI Features | AI 機能 | Title for AI features section in organization settings page |
| organization.compliance.ai.support.label | Allow AI-powered features | AI 機能を許可する | Label for AI support checkbox: when checked, AI-powered features are allowed for the organization |
| organization.delete.auto.renewal.warning | Deleting this organization will also disable the auto-renewal of your subscription. | この組織を削除すると、サブスクリプションの自動更新も無効になります。 | Info text for organization deletion when auto-renewal is enabled |
| organization.delete.warning | Deleting this organization will suspend it immediately and permanently remove all its resources, including projects, users, and all associated review tasks after 14 days. You can cancel the deletion during the suspension period. | この組織を削除すると、即座に停止され、14日後にプロジェクト、ユーザー、および関連するすべてのレビュータスクを含むすべてのリソースが永続的に削除されます。停止期間中は削除をキャンセルできます。 | Warning text for organization deletion |
| organization.deletion.no.access.message | You can no longer access your account data unless the deletion is cancelled. | 削除をキャンセルしない限り、アカウントデータにアクセスできません。 | Common message about no longer being able to access account data |

| organization.display.name.update.error | Failed to update organization name | 組織名の更新に失敗しました | Error message presented when organization display name update fails. |
| organization.display.name.update.success | Organization name updated successfully | 組織名を更新しました | Message presented when organization display name update succeeds. |
| organization.general.info | General Information | 一般情報 | Title for general organization information section |
| organization.general.info.description | This information will be shown to users before they authenticate in your organization. | この情報は、ユーザーが組織で認証する前に表示されます。 | Description text explaining what organization information is used for |
| organization.id.cannot.end.with.hyphen | Organization ID cannot end with a hyphen | 組織IDはハイフンで終わることはできません | Error message when organization ID ends with hyphen |

| organization.id.format.info | Use lowercase letters (a-z), digits (0-9), or hyphens (-). | 小文字 (a-z)、数字 (0-9)、またはハイフン (-) を使用してください。 | Info text explaining the format requirements for organization ID |
| organization.id.invalid | Use lowercase letters, digits, and hyphens only (3-63 chars) | 小文字、数字、ハイフンのみ使用 (3-63文字) | Error message for invalid organization ID format |
| organization.id.invalid.characters | Organization ID can only contain lowercase letters (a-z), digits (0-9), and hyphens (-). | 組織IDには小文字 (a-z)、数字 (0-9)、ハイフン (-) のみ使用できます。 | Error message when organization ID contains invalid characters |
| organization.id.is.unavailable | This organization ID is unavailable. Please choose a different ID or contact us at support@oxygenxml.com | この組織IDは利用できません。別のIDを選択するか、support@oxygenxml.comまでお問い合わせください | Generic anti-enumeration message when organization ID cannot be used (reserved term, protected brand mismatch, or already exists). |
| organization.id.not.permitted | This organization ID is not permitted | この組織IDは許可されていません | Error message when organization ID is not permitted (e.g., default organization in Cloud deployment) |
| organization.id.required | Organization ID is required | 組織IDが必要です | Error message when organization ID is required but not provided |
| organization.id.too.long | Organization ID cannot exceed 63 characters | 組織IDは63文字を超えることはできません | Error message when organization ID is too long |
| organization.id.too.short | Organization ID must be at least 3 characters long | 組織IDは3文字以上である必要があります | Error message when organization ID is too short |
| organization.id.url.info | Your organization ID will be part of your URL: {url} | 組織IDはURLの一部になります：{url} | Info text explaining that organization ID will be part of the URL |
| organization.image.change.tooltip | Change organization image | 組織画像を変更 | Tooltip and aria-label for the organization image change button |
| organization.image.delete.confirm.message | Are you sure you want to remove the current organization image? The avatar will return to showing the organization initials. | 現在の組織画像を削除してもよろしいですか？アバターは組織のイニシャルの表示に戻ります。 |  |

| organization.image.delete.success | Organization image deleted successfully. | 組織の画像が正常に削除されました。 |  |

| organization.image.help.text | For best quality, use square images of at least 128x128 pixels. Limit: 100KB. | 最高品質にするには、少なくとも128x128ピクセルの正方形画像を使用してください。制限：100KB。 |  |

| organization.image.remove | Remove | 削除 |  |

| organization.image.title | Organization Image | 組織画像 |  |
| organization.image.unsupported.format | Unsupported file format. Please use PNG, JPEG, or WebP. | サポートされていないファイル形式です。PNG、JPEG、またはWebPを使用してください。 |  |

| project.type.dita.tooltip | DITA is for maps, topics, and related DITA files. With this type, you can organize publications, publish to PDF and WebHelp, review DITA quality in Problems, reuse content with keys and components, run translation jobs, search within the current map, and insert images and media from the Content library. | DITAはマップ、トピック、関連するDITAファイル向けです。この種類では、パブリケーションの整理、PDFおよびWebHelpへのパブリッシュ、問題でのDITA品質チェック、キーとコンポーネントによるコンテンツ再利用、翻訳ジョブ、現在のマップ内の検索、コンテンツライブラリからの画像やメディアの挿入ができます。 | Shown on the DITA option when choosing project type, and on the type in settings. |
| project.type.generic | Generic | 汎用 | Project type option for non-DITA content. DITA is the acronym and is not translated. |
| project.type.generic.tooltip | Generic is for projects that are not DITA. With this type, you can store any mix of files, including other XML formats such as DocBook or S1000D, Markdown, and non-XML documents. You can edit files, create review tasks, and search across the project. DITA-specific features are not available, including publications, PDF and WebHelp publishing, DITA quality checks in Problems, content reuse with keys and components, translation jobs, search within the current map, and inserting images and media from the Content library. | 汎用はDITAではないプロジェクト向けです。この種類では、DocBookやS1000Dなどの他のXML形式、Markdown、非XMLドキュメントなど、任意のファイルを混在できます。ファイルの編集、レビュータスクの作成、プロジェクト全体の検索ができます。パブリケーション、PDFおよびWebHelpのパブリッシュ、問題でのDITA品質チェック、キーとコンポーネントによるコンテンツ再利用、翻訳ジョブ、現在のマップ内の検索、コンテンツライブラリからの画像やメディアの挿入など、DITA固有の機能は利用できません。 | Shown on the Generic option when choosing project type, and on the type in settings. |

| role_change_info_author | As an Author, you now have access to the Projects functionality, including the ability to create and manage projects. | 著者として、プロジェクト機能へのアクセス権が付与され、プロジェクトの作成や管理が可能になりました。 | Info for users who become AUTHOR. |
| role_change_info_contributor | As a Contributor, you can be assigned as a collaborator on projects to suggest changes to their files by creating review tasks from your suggestions. | コントリビューターとして、プロジェクトの共同作業者として割り当てられ、提案から作成したレビュー任務によってファイルへの変更を提案できます。 | Info for users who become CONTRIBUTOR, coming from a role other than AUTHOR. |
| role_change_info_contributor_from_author | As a Contributor, your access to the Projects functionality is now limited to that of a project collaborator: you can be assigned as a collaborator on projects to suggest changes to their files by creating review tasks from your suggestions. | コントリビューターとして、プロジェクト機能へのアクセスは、プロジェクトの共同作業者としてのアクセスに限定されます。提案から作成したレビュー任務によって、プロジェクトの共同作業者として割り当てられ、ファイルへの変更を提案できます。 | Info for users who become CONTRIBUTOR after previously being AUTHOR: unlike SME, they keep some project access, just limited to what a project collaborator has. |
| role_change_info_none | Your platform role has been set to "No license seat". You will retain administrator access to your organization settings, but will not consume a paid license seat. | プラットフォームロールが「ライセンスなし」に設定されました。組織設定への管理者アクセスは維持されますが、有料ライセンス席は消費されません。 | Info for users who are demoted to NONE (no platform role, no license seat consumed). |
| role_change_info_sme | As a Subject Matter Expert, you can create and manage review tasks. | SME（専門家）として、レビュー任務を作成および管理できます。 | Info for users who become SME, coming from a role other than AUTHOR. |
| role_change_info_sme_from_author | As a Subject Matter Expert, you no longer have access to the Projects functionality. | SME（専門家）として、プロジェクト機能へのアクセス権がなくなりました。 | Info for users who become SME after previously being AUTHOR. |
| role_change_info_sme_from_contributor | As a Subject Matter Expert, you still have access to review tasks, but no longer have access to the projects you were a collaborator on. | SME（専門家）として、レビュー任務へのアクセスは引き続き可能ですが、共同作業者として参加していたプロジェクトへのアクセス権はなくなりました。 | Info for users who become SME after previously being CONTRIBUTOR: they keep working with review tasks, but lose the project-collaborator access they had (SME has no project access at all). |

| save_new_external_resources_message | The task contains modified documents that were originally stored on a remote server, as well as new documents.\n The modified documents are opened in separate editor tabs. You need to manually save them (according to the protocol required by the remote server).\n The newly added documents must be saved locally (click "Choose directory" to save them) and then manually uploaded on the remote server. | タスクには、元々リモート サーバー上に保存されていたドキュメントを変更したドキュメントと、新規ドキュメントが含まれます。\n変更されたドキュメントは、個別のエディター タブで開かれています。これらを手動で保存する必要があります (リモート サーバーにより要求されるプロトコルに従ってください)。\n新規に追加するドキュメントはローカルに保存し ([Choose directory (ディレクトリの選択)] をクリックして保存します)、次に、リモート サーバーに手動でアップロードしてください。 | Message presented to the user when there are new external resources are applied. |
| save_new_external_resources_title | Save new documents | 新規ドキュメントの保存 | Title of the dialog presented to the user when there are new external resources that can be saved in a local folder. |

| subscription.access.instructions.description | To manage your subscription, you'll need to access your 2Checkout account. Follow these steps: | サブスクリプションを管理するには、2Checkoutアカウントにアクセスする必要があります。以下の手順に従ってください： | Description for 2Checkout My Account access instructions |
